\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
Antiphonæ sequentes, ad lectionem Sacræ Scripturæ referuntur, quæ, sive ipsæ do\-mi\-ni\-cæ vel diebus
infra hebdomadam in Officio Lectionis Liturgiæ Horarum pro cyclo unius anni assignantur.\hfill\break
Loco tamen earum cani possunt antiphonæ de Evangelio quæ pro cantico {\reg Benedictus} iisdem dominicis assignantur.
}\vskip.4cm%

\vskip-.2cm
\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
Antiphonæ sequentes, ad lectionem Sacræ Scripturæ referuntur, quæ, sive ipsæ do\-mi\-ni\-cæ
vel diebus infra hebdomadam in Officio Lectionis Liturgiæ Horarum pro cyclo duorum
annorum assignantur.\hfill\break
Loco tamen earum cani possunt antiphonæ de Evangelio quæ pro cantico {\reg Benedictus}
iisdem dominicis assignantur.
}\vskip.4cm%
\eject
\goodbreak\line{\hskip-4.6cm\hbox to 14.7cm{\mmed\hfill Anno I\hfill}\hss}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%

%Dixit Dominus mulieri
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Dixit Dominus mulieri Chananææ: Non est bonum sumere panem filiorum et mittere canibus ad manducandum. Utique, Domine, nam et catelli edunt de micis quæ cadunt de mensa dominorum suorum. Ait illi Iesus: Mulier, magna est fides tua: fiat tibi sicut petisti.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\pdfextension dest name {DOM129} fit
%Dixit autem dominus servo
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Dixit autem dominus servo: Redde quod debes. Procidens autem servus ille rogabat eum dicens: Patientiam habe in me, et omnia reddam tibi.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Serve nequam
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Serve nequam, omne debitum dimisi tibi, quoniam rogasti me; nonne ergo oportuit et te misereri conservi tui, sicut et ego tui misertus sum?\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Simile est enim... patrifamilias
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Simile est enim regnum cælorum homini patrifamilias, qui exiit primo mane conducere operarios in vineam suam, dicit Dominus.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Dixit paterfamilias operariis suis: Quid hic statis tota die otiosi? At illi respondentes dixerunt: Quia nemo nos conduxit. Ite in vineam meam, et quod iustum fuerit dabo vobis.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Domine non sum dignus
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Domine, non sum dignus ut intres sub tectum meum, sed tantum dic verbo, et sanabitur puer meus.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vskip.4cm
%Nam et ego
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Nam et ego homo sum sub potestate constitutus, habens sub me milites; et dico huic: Vade, et vadit; et servo meo: Fac hoc, et facit.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\pdfextension dest name {DOM181} fit
%Adulescens tibi dico
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Adulescens, tibi dico: Surge. Et resedit qui erat mortuus et cœpit loqui; et dedit illum matri suæ.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Accepit autem omnes timor, et magnificabant Deum dicentes: Quia propheta magnus surrexit in nobis, et quia Deus visitavit plebem suam.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\pdfextension dest name {DOM182} fit
%In diebus illis
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{In diebus illis mulier quæ erat in civitate peccatrix, ut cognovit quod Iesus accubuit in domo Simonis leprosi, attulit alabastrum unguenti; et stans retro secus pedes Domini Iesu, lacrimis cœpit rigare pedes eius et capillis capitis sui tergebat; et osculabatur pedes eius et unguento ungebat.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Ecce ascendimus Ierosolymam, et consummabuntur omnia quæ scripta sunt de Filio hominis: tradetur enim gentibus, et illudetur et flagellabitur et conspuetur; et postquam flagellaverint, occident eum; et die tertia resurget.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Dum intraret Iesus quoddam castellum, occurrerunt ei decem viri leprosi; qui steterunt a longe et levaverunt vocem dicentes: Iesu præceptor, miserere nostri.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Lc 17,12-13; }{CAO 2458; ARII 383}{B}{Laudes}{A[28D,L,Bc]}%
\greannotation{\med 1d2}%
\gabcsnippet{(c4)Dum(c) in(cd~)trá(d)ret(d) Ie(f)sus(ddo) quod(c)dam(d) ca(f)stél(d)lum,(df/ddoc) (,) oc(f)cur(g)ré(gh)runt(gf~) e(g)i(d) de(g)cem(f) vi(fe)ri(d) le(fe)pró(de)si;(c) (;) qui(f) ste(g)té(ghGF)runt(g) a(gh) lon(fg>)ge(f) (,) et(f) le(f)va(f)vé(fg)runt(f) vo(ef)cem(g>) di(fe)cén(d)tes:(dfd/doc) (;) Ie(f)su(ixfhg/hih) præ(g)cép(fe)tor,(c) (,) mi(e)se(f)ré(g)re(fe) no(d)stri.(d) (::) E(h) u(h) o(g) u(f) a(g) e.(gvFED) (::)}%
\vskip.4cm
%Nonne decem mundati
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Nonne decem mundati sunt? et novem ubi sunt? Non est inventus qui rediret et daret gloriam Deo, nisi hic alienigena. Vade, quia fides tua te salvum fecit, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
\ococommentary{Lc 17,17.18.19; }{CAO 3945; ARII 383}{M}{II Vesp.}{A[28D,V,Mc]}%

\pdfextension dest name {DOM200} fit
%Omnis qui petit
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Omnis qui petit, accipit; et qui quærit, invenit; pulsanti aperietur, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vskip.4cm
%Dum venerit Filius
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Dum venerit Filius hominis, putas, inveniet fidem super terram?\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Stans a longe
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Stans a longe publicanus nolebat oculos ad cælum levare, sed percutiebat pectus suum dicens: Deus, propitius esto mihi peccatori.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Descendit hic iustificatus
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Descendit hic iustificatus in domum suam ab illo: quia omnis qui se exaltat, humiliabitur; et qui se humiliat, exaltabitur.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\pdfextension dest name {DOM202} fit
%Zachæe festinans
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Zachæe, festinans descende, quia hodie in domo tua oportet me manere. At ille festinans descendit et suscepit illum gaudens in domum suam. Hodie huic domui salus a Deo facta est, alleluia.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Dic nobis quando hæc
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Dic nobis, quando hæc erunt, Domine, aut quod signum adventus tui? Videte, ne quis vos seducat ullo modo.
